type: ignore[assignment]\n    placeholders_replaced: Counter = None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n    missing_keys: Counter = None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        if self.placeholders_seen is None:\n            self.placeholders_seen = Counter()\n        if self.placeholders_replaced is None:\n            self.placeholders_replaced = Counter()\n        if self.missing_keys is None:\n            self.missing_keys = Counter()\n\n\ndef load_json(path: Path) -> Dict[str, object]:\n    with path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"Expected top-level JSON object, got: {type(data).__name__}\")\n    return data\n\n\ndef extract_hwpx(src: Path, dst_dir: Path) -> None:\n    if dst_dir.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(dst_dir)\n    dst_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(src, \"r\") as z:\n        z.extractall(dst_dir)\n\n\ndef iter_content_xml_files(root: Path) -> List[Path]:\n    # HWPX often stores body under Contents/section*.xml.\n    contents = root / \"Contents\"\n    if not contents.exists():\n        return []\n    return sorted([p for p in contents.glob(\"section*.xml\") if p.is_file()])\n\n\ndef register_namespaces_from_root(root_elem: ET.Element) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Register namespaces so ElementTree preserves prefixes on write.\"\"\"\n    # ElementTree doesn't expose xmlns declarations directly, but root tag contains {uri}local.\n    # We also scan attributes for xmlns:* style declarations if present.\n    namespaces: Dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    # Heuristic: known HWPML prefix is typically hp.\n    if root_elem.tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        uri = root_elem.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[0][1:]\n        namespaces[\"hp\"] = uri\n\n    for k, v in root_elem.attrib.items():\n        if k == \"xmlns\":\n            namespaces[\"\"] = v\n        elif k.startswith(\"{http://www.w3.org/2000/xmlns/}\"):\n            prefix = k.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n            namespaces[prefix] = v\n\n    # Register what we found (and ensure hp is registered if present)\n    for prefix, uri in namespaces.items():\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n    return namespaces\n\n\ndef _ns_tag(ns_uri: str, local: str) -> str:\n    return f\"{{{ns_uri}}}{local}\"\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, data: Dict[str, object], stats: ReplaceStats) -> str:\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        stats.placeholders_seen[key] += 1\n        if key in data:\n            val = data[key]\n            # Convert JSON scalars to their string representation\n            stats.placeholders_replaced[key] += 1\n            return \"\" if val is None else str(val)\n        stats.missing_keys[key] += 1\n        # Replace missing keys with a marker that does not include braces\n        return f\"[누락:{key}]\"\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n\n\ndef process_section_xml(path: Path, data: Dict[str, object], stats: ReplaceStats) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Returns (changed, errors).\"\"\"\n    errors: List[str] = []\n\n    raw = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    try:\n        tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(raw))\n    except ET.ParseError as e:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"XML parse error in {path}: {e}\")\n\n    root = tree.getroot()\n    nsmap = register_namespaces_from_root(root)\n\n    hp_uri = nsmap.get(\"hp\")\n    if not hp_uri:\n        # Fall back to known HWPML URIs if not detected\n        # (task file uses 2010/HWPML)\n        hp_uri = \"http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML\"\n        ET.register_namespace(\"hp\", hp_uri)\n\n    p_tag = _ns_tag(hp_uri, \"p\")\n    t_tag = _ns_tag(hp_uri, \"t\")\n    linesegarray_tag = _ns_tag(hp_uri, \"linesegarray\")\n\n    changed_any = False\n\n    for p in root.iter(p_tag):\n        # Collect all <hp:t> descendants within this paragraph.\n        t_elems = list(p.iter(t_tag))\n        if not t_elems:\n            continue\n\n        concat = \"\".join([(te.text or \"\") for te in t_elems])\n        if \"{{\" not in concat and \"}}\" not in concat:\n            continue\n\n        replaced = replace_placeholders_in_text(concat, data, stats)\n        if replaced == concat:\n            # Contains braces but didn't match our placeholder pattern; treat as error but do not alter.\n            errors.append(f\"Unrecognized placeholder-like text in {path.name} paragraph id={p.get('id')}: {concat!r}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Update first <hp:t>, clear subsequent ones.\n        t_elems[0].text = replaced\n        for te in t_elems[1:]:\n            te.text = \"\"\n\n        # Remove layout cache from modified paragraph to prevent overlaps.\n        for lsa in list(p.findall(f\"./{linesegarray_tag}\")):\n            p.remove(lsa)\n\n        changed_any = True\n        stats.paragraphs_modified += 1\n\n    if changed_any:\n        # (This template is minimal; the above already captures all files under src_dir.)\n\n\ndef validate_hwpx(path: Path) -> None:\n    if VALIDATE_DIR.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(VALIDATE_DIR)\n    VALIDATE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(path, \"r\") as z:\n        infos = z.infolist()\n        if not infos:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Output HWPX is empty\")\n\n        first = infos[0]\n        if first.filename != MIMETYPE_FILENAME:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"ZIP first entry must be '{MIMETYPE_FILENAME}', got: {first.filename}\")\n        if first.compress_type != zipfile.ZIP_STORED:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"mimetype must be stored uncompressed (ZIP_STORED)\")\n\n        mimetype_bytes = z.read(MIMETYPE_FILENAME)\n        try:\n            mimetype_txt = mimetype_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\")\n        except UnicodeDecodeError:\n            mimetype_txt = mimetype_bytes.decode(\"latin-1\")\n        if mimetype_txt != MIMETYPE_VALUE:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"mimetype content mismatch: {mimetype_txt!r}\")\n\n        z.extractall(VALIDATE_DIR)\n\n    #
